ebox[6.72252pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47736pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.68549pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\alpha_{i}}{\beta_{i}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.38687pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.38687pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.84033pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.94475pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.72252pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.72252pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47736pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.68549pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\alpha_{i}}{\beta_{i}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.38687pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.38687pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.84033pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.94475pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.72252pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.72252pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47736pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.68549pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,,

(C.4)

and the element
x_{0}
is understood to contain terms that do not couple to the background. The multi-indexed field
\mathchoice{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.81232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.81232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.20248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.35892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.95927pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.95927pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.67052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.97893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.25pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.25pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.67499pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.05043pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.05043pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.65828pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.19739pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.19739pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.12631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43472pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu_{1}}{\nu_{1}}{\cdots}{\mu_{i}}{\nu_{i}}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.81232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.81232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.20248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.35892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.95927pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.95927pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.67052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.97893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.25pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.25pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.67499pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.05043pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.05043pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.65828pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.19739pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.19739pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.12631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43472pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu_{1}}{\nu_{1}}{\cdots}{\mu_{i}}{\nu_{i}}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.81232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.81232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.20248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.35892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.95927pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.95927pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.67052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.97893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.25pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.25pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.67499pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.05043pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.05043pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.65828pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.19739pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.19739pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.12631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43472pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu_{1}}{\nu_{1}}{\cdots}{\mu_{i}}{\nu_{i}}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.81232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.81232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.20248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.35892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.95927pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.95927pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.67052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.97893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.25pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.25pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.67499pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.05043pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.05043pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.65828pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81471pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.19739pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.19739pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.12631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.43472pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu_{1}}{\nu_{1}}{\cdots}{\mu_{i}}{\nu_{i}}}}}
corresponds to matter and is conventionally assumed to be independent of the metric. Based on the equation above, we will now categorise each case based on a finite number of building blocks and their corresponding index structure. For simplicity, we will always consider only one family of fields with a fixed number of indices i.e. we shall pick an element from the following set

\displaystyle\quantity{\tensor{X}{_{\mu_{1}}_{\mu_{2}}},\dots,\tensor{X}{_{\mu_{1}}_{\mu_{2}}_{\mu_{3}\cdots}}}\,,

(C.5)

and start building scalars with it. One reason for this choice is that any combination of fields with a different (or not) number of indices can be a special case of a field whose number of indices is equal to the sum of the number of indices of the independent fields that enter the combination we just mentioned. Another reason is that a potential combination of fields with a different number of indices, effectively increases the degrees of freedom of a theory which will result in significantly more complicated equations.

Let us now consider only one family of fields that can be parametrised by
\mathchoice{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}

x_{i}\coloneqq\mathchoice{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}=\tr(X_{i})\,,

(C.7)

which leads to the following properties

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\quantity(X_{i+j})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity(X_{i+j})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity(X_{i+j})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity(X_{i+j})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}=\mathchoice{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\lambda}}}}\mathchoice{\quantity(X_{j})^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity(X_{j})^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity(X_{j})^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity(X_{j})^{{{\lambda}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.74307pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.85834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}\,,\quad\mathchoice{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity(X_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}=\mathchoice{\quantity([X]^{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity([X]^{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity([X]^{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\quantity([X]^{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}\,,\quad i,j\in\mathds{N}^{+}\,,

(C.8)

and consequently we may rewrite (
C.3
) as

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}=\mathcal{L}\quantity(x_{0},\tr[X],\dots,\tr[X^{d}])\,,

(C.9)

where
D
is now reduced to
d
as a direct consequence of (
B.7
). This was first noticed in
[
66
]
for the special antisymmetric case of
\mathchoice{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}
but for now, we choose to remain agnostic about any symmetries. Let us also note that these properties are generally true and independent of the nature of the deformation.

C.1
Deformations with determinants

In this section, we consider the following family of deformations

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}=\int\differential[d]{x}\sqrt{-g}\hskip 2.84544pt\alpha[\det\mathchoice{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}]^{1/\beta}\,,

(C.10)

where
\mathchoice{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}
is the energy stress-tensor of the deformed theory
\mathcal{L}
and
\alpha,\beta
are arbitrary constants. We should note that if
\beta>d
the deformation is relevant, if
\beta=d
it is marginal and finally, if
\beta<d
the deformation is irrelevant. From (
C.2
), we have

\displaystyle\det\mathchoice{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}=\det(\mathcal{L}\tensor{\de}{^\mu_\nu}-2\tensor{\Sigma}{^\mu_\nu})\,.

(C.11)

The advantage of this “one index upstairs, one index downstairs” notation is that one does not have to contract with the (inverse) metric in order to calculate traces. This enables us to use formula (
B.6
)

\displaystyle\det\mathchoice{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}=\sum_{n=0}^{\rank\Sigma}c_{n}\mathcal{L}^{d-n}\,,\qquad c_{n}=(-1)^{n}e_{n}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},\dots,\rho_{d})\,,

(C.12)

where it has become evident that the complexity of the expression is directly connected to the rank of
\Sigma
. This was noticed for the first time in
[
4
]
for the
\rank\Sigma=1
case and explained in more detail in
[
63
]
. Furthermore, since calculating the eigenvalues of
\Sigma
is not an easy task we can use the following formula

\displaystyle e_{n}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},\dots,\rho_{d})=\frac{(-1)^{n}}{n!}B_{n}(-p_{1},-1!p_{2},-2!p_{3},\dots,-(n-1)!p_{n})\,,\qquad p_{n}\coloneqq\tr(\Sigma^{n})\,,

(C.13)

where
B_{n}
is the
n^{\text{th}}
complete exponential Bell polynomial, thus (
C.12
) can take the following form

\displaystyle\det\mathchoice{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}=\sum_{n=0}^{\rank\Sigma}c_{n}\quantity[\tr(\Sigma),\tr(\Sigma^{2}),\dots,\tr(\Sigma^{n})]\mathcal{L}^{d-n}\,,

(C.14)

where we explicitly state the dependence of the coefficient
c_{n}
on the traces of the first
n
powers of
\Sigma
. Looking back to (
C.1
) we observe that slowly, a non-linear yet first order partial differential equation is forming and the struggle to keep it under control comes not only from the rank of
\Sigma
but also from its power trace structure. Up to this point
\mathcal{L}
may contain an arbitrary number of matter fields from (
C.5
). Proceeding with the two index family, a quick calculation shows

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\Sigma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\Sigma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\Sigma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\Sigma^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\frac{i}{2}\partialderivative{\mathcal{L}}{x_{i}}\mathchoice{(X_{i}+X^{\text{T}}_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{(X_{i}+X^{\text{T}}_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{(X_{i}+X^{\text{T}}_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{(X_{i}+X^{\text{T}}_{i})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}\,,\quad\tr(\Sigma)=\sum_{i=1}^{d}i\partialderivative{\mathcal{L}}{x_{i}}x_{i}\,,\quad\mathchoice{(X^{\text{T}}_{k})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{(X^{\text{T}}_{k})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{(X^{\text{T}}_{k})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{(X^{\text{T}}_{k})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}\coloneqq\mathchoice{(X_{k})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(X_{k})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(X_{k})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(X_{k})^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,,

(C.15)

and the trace of the
n^{\text{th}}
power of
\Sigma
is given by the following non-trivial expression

\displaystyle\tr(\Sigma^{n})=\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{n}=1}^{d}\frac{i_{1}\cdots i_{n}}{2^{n}}\partialderivative{\mathcal{L}}{x_{i_{1}}}\cdots\partialderivative{\mathcal{L}}{x_{i_{n}}}\tr([X_{i_{1}}+X^{\text{T}}_{i_{1}}]\cdots[X_{i_{n}}+X^{\text{T}}_{i_{n}}])\,,

(C.16)

which reduces to

\displaystyle\tr(\Sigma^{n})=\hskip-5.69046pt\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{n}=1}^{d}\quantity[\prod_{k=1}^{n}i_{k}\pdv{\mathcal{L}}{x_{i_{k}}}]x_{i_{1}+\cdots+i_{n}}\,,\quad\tr(\Sigma^{n})=\hskip-5.69046pt\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{n}=1}^{\lfloor\frac{d}{2}\rfloor}\quantity[\prod_{k=1}^{n}2i_{k}\pdv{\mathcal{L}}{x_{2i_{k}}}]x_{2i_{1}+\cdots+2i_{n}}\,,

(C.17)

when
\mathchoice{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}
is symmetric and antisymmetric respectively. In the case of mixed symmetry, the expression is no longer a function of
x_{i}
and
\partial_{x_{i}}\mathcal{L}
, hence this approach may not apply.

C.2
Maxwell’s theory

We now follow with a minimal working example. Setting
d=3
and picking
\mathchoice{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}
to be antisymmetric leads to
\mathcal{L}=\mathcal{L}(x_{0},x_{2})
and it follows that
\rank\Sigma=2
. Furthermore, we set
\alpha=1
and
\beta=2
in (
C.10
). Then the combination of (
B.7
,
C.1
,
C.10
) and (
C.17
) leads to

\displaystyle\partial_{\lambda}\mathcal{L}=\sqrt{\mathcal{L}}\absolutevalue{\mathcal{L}-2x_{2}\partial_{x_{2}}\mathcal{L}}\,,\qquad\mathcal{L}_{0}=x_{2}\,,

(C.18)

where the absolute value comes from the square root. Picking either the positive
(+)
or negative
(-)
branch we reach the following solutions

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\text{d}}=\frac{2\quantity(1\pm\sqrt{x_{2}}\la+\sqrt{1\pm 2\sqrt{x_{2}}\la})}{\lambda^{2}}\,,\quad\mathcal{L}_{\text{c}}=\frac{2\quantity(1\pm\sqrt{x_{2}}\la-\sqrt{1\pm 2\sqrt{x_{2}}\la})}{\lambda^{2}}\,.

(C.19)

The final step is to identify
4x_{2}
with
-\mathchoice{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}\mathchoice{F^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{F^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\text . It is worth stressing that only the
\mathcal{L}_{\text{c}}
set of solutions has a well-defined
\lambda\rightarrow 0
limit whereas
\mathcal{L}_{\text{d}}
diverges to
+\infty
respectively. Furthermore, it is important to note that (
C.19
) is the deformed Lagrangian density of any matter action that is constructed via the antisymmetric
\mathchoice{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}
field with
\mathcal{L}_{0}=x_{2}
. In other words, the identification
\mathchoice{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}\leftrightarrow\mathchoice{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\texts is not necessary as far as the deformation is concerned.

C.3
Quadratic and root deformations of sigma models

For this section we set
d=2
and we consider the deformation given by (
3.12
) i.e.

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}=\int\differential[2]{x}\sqrt{-g}\hskip 2.84544pt2b_{0}\quantity{-\det\tensor{T}{^\mu_\nu}+\frac{b_{2}}{\la'}\tensor{T}{^\mu_\mu}-\frac{b_{2}(1+b_{2})}{{\la'}^{2}}}\,,

(C.20)

where
\lambda^{\prime}\coloneqq 2\lambda b_{0}
. Notice that for
b_{0}=1/2
and
b_{2}=0
the operator reduces to
T\overline{T}
. Another interesting albeit marginal operator is
[
20
]

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}=\int\differential[2]{x}\sqrt{-g}\hskip 1.42271pt\dfrac{b_{\text{r}}}{\sqrt{2}}\sqrt{\mathchoice{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{T^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}\mathchoice{T^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{T^{{{\nu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyl (C.21)

which is usually referred as the root-
T\overline{T}
deformation. As in the previous case,
b_{\text{r}}
is arbitrary and dimensionless. We will now proceed with the deformation of sigma models considering the two operators above. The non-linear
\sigma

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{0}=-\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{g^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$} }}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}{j}}}}\partial_{(\mu}\phi^{i}\partial_{\nu)}\phi^{j}\,,

(C.22)

where
V
is an arbitrary potential and
\mathcal{T}
corresponds to any topological term. From there we can identify
\mathchoice{\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}\leftrightarrow\mathchoice{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt where
\mathchoice{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}
is now symmetric and consequently
x_{1}\leftrightarrow\mathchoice{g^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{g^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}{\Phi^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}{\nu}}}}
. This leads to the following ansatz

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}=\mathcal{L}(\Omega,x_{0},x_{1},x_{2})\,,\qquad\Omega\coloneqq\sqrt{-g}\,.

(C.23)

Then, the combination of (
B.7
,
C.1
,
C.17
) and (
C.20
) leads to the following, highly non-trivial partial differential equation

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \partial_{\lambda^{\prime}}\mathcal{L}=(2x_{2}-x_{1}^{2})(\partial_{x_{1}}\mathcal{L}+2x_{1}\partial_{x_{2}}\mathcal{L})^{2}-\quantity(\mathfrak{d}\mathcal{L}-\dfrac{b_{2}}{\lambda' })^{2}-\frac{b_{2}}{{\lambda^{\prime}}^{2}}\,,\end{aligned}

(C.24)

where
\mathfrak{d}\coloneqq 1-x_{1}\partial_{x_{1}}-2x_{2}\partial_{x_{2}}+\Omega\partial_{\Omega}
. Similarly, for (
C.21
) we get

\displaystyle\partial_{\mu}\mathcal{L}=b_{\text{r}}\sqrt{2x_{2}-x_{1}^{2}}(\partial_{x_{1}}\mathcal{L}+2x_{1}\partial_{x_{2}}\mathcal{L})\,,

(C.25)

where
b_{\text{r}}
has absorbed a factor of
\pm
coming from the square root and
\mu
is the dimensionless deformation parameter for the root-
T\overline{T}
operator. An interesting fact about the equation above is that all
\sqrt{-g}
related terms cancel exactly, something that is clearly not true in (
C.24
). Equation (
C.25
) can be solved exactly
[
20
]
i.e.

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}=\mathcall{L}_{0}\quantity(\Omega,x_{0},x_{1,\m},x_{2,\m})\,,

(C.26)

where

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &x_{1,\mu}=x_{1}\cosh(b_{\text{r}}\m)+\sqrt{2x_{2}-x_{1}^{2}}\sinh(b_{\text{r}}\m)\,,\\
&x_{2,\mu}=x_{2}\cosh(2b_{\text{r}}\m)+x_{1}\sqrt{2x_{2}-x_{1}^{2}}\sinh(2b_{\text{r}}\m)\,,\end{aligned}

(C.27)

with
x_{2,\mu}-x_{1,\mu}^{2}=x_{2}-x_{1}^{2}
and
\mathcall{L}_{0}
being any function. On the other hand, equation (
C.24
) together with (
C.22
) gives

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\pm}=\dfrac{1+b_{2}}{\lambda^{\prime}}-\dfrac{1}{2\widetilde{\lambda^{\prime}}}\quantity(1\pm\sqrt{1+2\widetilde{\la'}x_{1,\m}+2{\widetilde{\la'}}^{2}\qty(x_{1}^{2}-x_{2})})+\dfrac{\mathcal{T}}{\sqrt{-g}}\,,

(C.28)

where
\widetilde{\lambda^{\prime}}\coloneqq\lambda^{\prime}(1-V\lambda^{\prime})
and
x_{1}
is promoted to
x_{1,\mu}
in accordance with (
C.26
) and (
C.27
). One can easily check that (
C.28
) satisfies (
C.24
) and (
C.25
) simultaneously. Moreover, the limit
\mu\rightarrow 0
is always regular, but the limit
\lambda\rightarrow 0
diverges to
\pm\infty
with the exception of
b_{2}=0
and
\mathcal{L}_{+}
, or
b_{2}=-1
and
\mathcal{L}_{-}
. In the first case we recover (
C.22
) with
x_{1}\rightarrow x_{1,\mu}
and in the second
x_{1,\mu}/2+\mathcal{T}/\sqrt{-g}
. Finally, the two flows commute since (
C.24
) retains its form after the change of coordinates:
x_{1}\rightarrow x_{1,\mu}
and
x_{2}\rightarrow x_{2,\mu}
.

Appendix D
Deformed energy spectrum

In this final appendix, we will derive the energy spectrum of the operator (
3.2
) taking into account only the first constraint (
3.8
). The operator now becomes

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}=\int\differential[2]{x}\sqrt{-g}\hskip 1.42271pt\quantity{-2b_{0}\det\tensor{T}{^\mu_\nu}+\frac{b_{2}}{\la}\tensor{T}{^\mu_\mu}+\frac{b_{3}}{\la^{2}}}\,.

(D.1)

As in
[
1
]
, we place our theory on a Euclidean cylinder of circumference
R
with coordinates
(\tau,x)\sim(\tau,x+R)
and
\tau=it
. After Wick rotating, (
C.1
) can be written as

\displaystyle\partial_{\lambda}\log\mathcal{Z}_{\text{E}}=-\expectationvalue{\mathcal{O}_{\text{E}}}\,,

(D.2)

where the subscript E denotes the Euclidean version of the corresponding quantity. Please do keep in mind that (
D.2
) is generally true, and is independent of the operator (
D.1
) or the cylinder. In our case it follows

\displaystyle\partial_{\lambda}E_{n}(\lambda,R)=2b_{0}R\expectationvalue{\det\tensor*{T}{_{\text{E}}^\mu_\nu}}{n}-\dfrac{b_{2}R}{\lambda}\expectationvalue{\tensor*{T}{_{\text{E}}^\mu_\mu}}{n}-\dfrac{b_{3}R}{\lambda^{2}}\,,

(D.3)

and since
10
10

10

For a detailed derivation of the pressure term one may refer to
[
67
]
.

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{\tensor{T}{_\ta_\ta}}{n}=-\dfrac{E_{n}(\lambda,R)}{R}\,,\quad\expectationvalue{\tensor{T}{_\ta_x}}{n}=-\dfrac{iP_{n}(\lambda,R)}{R}\,,\quad\expectationvalue{\tensor{T}{_x_x}}{n}=-\partialderivative{E_{n}(\la,R)}{R}\,,

(D.4)

we reach the following equation

\displaystyle\partial_{\lambda}E_{n}=2b_{0}\quantity(E_{n}\pdv{E_{n}}{R}+\dfrac{P_{n}^{2}}{R})+\dfrac{b_{2}}{\lambda}\quantity(E_{n}+R\pdv{E_{n}}{R})-\dfrac{b_{3}R}{\lambda^{2}}\,,

(D.5)

where the
\lambda,R
dependence is suppressed. It is rather interesting that (
D.5
) does not seem to have the form of the inviscid Burgers’ equation. Nevertheless, it can still be solved via the following dimensionless quantities
[
68
,
69
]

\displaystyle A_{n}(u)\coloneqq RE_{n}(\lambda,R)\,,\qquad\mathcal{P}_{n}\coloneqq RP_{n}(\lambda,R)\,,\qquad u\coloneqq\dfrac{\lambda}{R^{2}}\,,

(D.6)

where
\mathcal{P}_{n}
is a constant. This choice reduces (
D.5
) to an ordinary differential equation

\displaystyle(1+2b_{2}+4b_{0}uA_{n})A_{n}^{\prime}-2b_{0}(\mathcal{P}_{n}^{2}-A_{n}^{2})+\dfrac{b_{3}}{u^{2}}=0\,,\qquad A_{n}^{\prime}\coloneqq\derivative{A_{n}}{u}\,.

(D.7)

The solutions read

\displaystyle E^{\pm}_{n}(\lambda^{\prime},R)=-\dfrac{R\quantity(1+2b_{2})}{2\lambda^{\prime}}\pm\sqrt{\quantity(\dfrac{\mathcal{P}_{n}}{R})^{2}+\quantity(\dfrac{R\qty(1+2b_{2})}{2\la'})^{2}+\dfrac{2b_{0}\quantity(b_{3}R^{2}+\la'\mathcal{E}_{n})}{{\lambda^{\prime}}^{2}}}\,,

(D.8)

where
\mathcal{E}_{n}
is determined via an appropriate initial condition and
\mathcal{P}_{n}=2\pi p_{n}
, with
p_{n}\in\mathds{Z}
on the cylinder.

Appendix E
Some deformations and their coefficients

In this appendix, we will provide the deforming operators in five, six and seven dimensions along with a table that includes their higher order coefficients. In short, the deformation in five dimensions is given by

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \small\mathcal{O}=\int\differential[5]{x}f\hskip 1.42271pt\Bigg\{\lambda^{3}b_{0}\tr T^{5}+\lambda^{3}b_{1}\tr T^{4}\tr T+\lambda^{3}b_{2}\tr T^{3}\tr T^{2}+\lambda^{3}b_{3}\tr T^{3}(\tr T)^{2}+&\\[-5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{3}b_{4}(\tr T^{2})^{2}\tr T+\lambda^{3}b_{5}\tr T^{2}(\tr T)^{3}+\lambda^{3}b_{6}(\tr T)^{5}+\lambda^{2}b_{7}\tr T^{4}+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{2}b_{8}\tr T^{3}\tr T+\lambda^{2}b_{9}(\tr T^{2})^{2}+\lambda^{2}b_{10}\tr T^{2}(\tr T)^{2}+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{2}b_{11}(\tr T)^{4}+\lambda b_{12}\tr T^{3}+\lambda b_{13}\tr T^{2}\tr T+\lambda b_{14}(\tr T)^{3}+&\\
+\beta\quantity(\tr T^{2}-\dfrac{1}{4}(\tr T)^{2})+\dfrac{\beta_{l}}{\lambda}\tr T+\dfrac{\beta_{c}}{\lambda^{2}}&\Bigg\}\,,\end{aligned}

(E.1)

in six dimensions by

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \small\mathcal{O}=\int\differential[6]{x}f\hskip 1.42271pt\Bigg\{\lambda^{4}b_{0}\tr T^{6}+\lambda^{4}b_{1}\tr T^{5}\tr T+\lambda^{4}b_{2}\tr T^{4}\tr T^{2}+\lambda^{4}b_{3}\tr T^{4}(\tr T)^{2}+&\\[-5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{4}b_{4}(\tr T^{3})^{2}+\lambda^{4}b_{5}\tr T^{3}\tr T^{2}\tr T+\lambda^{4}b_{6}\tr T^{3}(\tr T)^{3}+\lambda^{4}b_{7}(\tr T^{2})^{3}+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{4}b_{8}(\tr T^{2})^{2}(\tr T)^{2}+\lambda^{4}b_{9}\tr T^{2}(\tr T)^{4}+\lambda^{4}b_{10}(\tr T)^{6}+\lambda^{3}b_{11}\tr T^{5}+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{3}b_{12}\tr T^{4}\tr T+\lambda^{3}b_{13}\tr T^{3}\tr T^{2}+\lambda^{3}b_{14}\tr T^{3}(\tr T)^{2}+\lambda^{3}b_{15}(\tr T^{2})^{2}\tr T+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{3}b_{16}\tr T^{2}(\tr T)^{3}+\lambda^{3}b_{17}(\tr T)^{5}+\lambda^{2}b_{18}\tr T^{4}+\lambda^{2}b_{19}\tr T^{3}\tr T+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{2}b_{20}(\tr T^{2})^{2}+\lambda^{2}b_{21}\tr T^{2}(\tr T)^{2}+\lambda^{2}b_{22}(\tr T)^{4}+\lambda b_{23}\tr T^{3}+&\\
+\lambda b_{24}\tr T^{2}\tr T+\lambda b_{25}(\tr T)^{3}+\beta\quantity(\tr T^{2}-\dfrac{1}{5}(\tr T)^{2})+\dfrac{\beta_{l}}{\lambda}\tr T+\dfrac{\beta_{c}}{\lambda^{2}}\Bigg\}&\,,\end{aligned}

(E.2)

and finally in seven dimensions the deformation reads

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \small\mathcal{O}=\int\differential[7]{x}f\hskip 1.42271pt\Bigg\{\lambda^{5}b_{0}\tr T^{7}+\lambda^{5}b_{1}\tr T^{6}\tr T+\lambda^{5}b_{2}\tr T^{5}\tr T^{2}+\lambda^{5}b_{3}\tr T^{5}(\tr T)^{2}+&\\[-5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{5}b_{4}\tr T^{4}\tr T^{3}+\lambda^{5}b_{5}\tr T^{4}\tr T^{2}\tr T+\lambda^{5}b_{6}\tr T^{4}(\tr T)^{3}+\lambda^{5}b_{7}(\tr T^{3})^{2}\tr T+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{5}b_{8}\tr T^{3}(\tr T^{2})^{2}+\lambda^{5}b_{9}\tr T^{3}\tr T^{2}(\tr T)^{2}+\lambda^{5}b_{10}\tr T^{3}(\tr T)^{4}+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{5}b_{11}(\tr T^{2})^{3}\tr T+\lambda^{5}b_{12}(\tr T^{2})^{2}(\tr T)^{3}+\lambda^{5}b_{13}\tr T^{2}(\tr T)^{5}+\lambda^{5}b_{14}(\tr T)^{7}+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{4}b_{15}\tr T^{6}+\lambda^{4}b_{16}\tr T^{5}\tr T+\lambda^{4}b_{17}\tr T^{4}\tr T^{2}+\lambda^{4}b_{18}\tr T^{4}(\tr T)^{2}+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{4}b_{19}(\tr T^{3})^{2}+\lambda^{4}b_{20}\tr T^{3}\tr T^{2}\tr T+\lambda^{4}b_{21}\tr T^{3}(\tr T)^{3}+\lambda^{4}b_{22}(\tr T^{2})^{3}+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{4}b_{23}(\tr T^{2})^{2}(\tr T)^{2}+\lambda^{4}b_{24}\tr T^{2}(\tr T)^{4}+\lambda^{4}b_{25}(\tr T)^{6}+\lambda^{3}b_{26}\tr T^{5}+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{3}b_{27}\tr T^{4}\tr T+\lambda^{3}b_{28}\tr T^{3}\tr T^{2}+\lambda^{3}b_{29}\tr T^{3}(\tr T)^{2}+\lambda^{3}b_{30}(\tr T^{2})^{2}\tr T+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{3}b_{31}\tr T^{2}(\tr T)^{3}+\lambda^{3}b_{32}(\tr T)^{5}+\lambda^{2}b_{33}\tr T^{4}+\lambda^{2}b_{34}\tr T^{3}\tr T+&\\[5.69046pt]
+\lambda^{2}b_{35}(\tr T^{2})^{2}+\lambda^{2}b_{36}\tr T^{2}(\tr T)^{2}+\lambda^{2}b_{37}(\tr T)^{4}+\lambda b_{38}\tr T^{3}+&\\
+\lambda b_{39}\tr T^{2}\tr T+\lambda b_{40}(\tr T)^{3}+\beta\quantity(\tr T^{2}-\dfrac{1}{6}(\tr T)^{2})+\dfrac{\beta_{l}}{\lambda}\tr T+\dfrac{\beta_{c}}{\lambda^{2}}\Bigg\}&\,.\end{aligned}

(E.3)

Table 1
:
All higher order coefficients are listed. We have defined
b_{0}\eqqcolon a^{d-2}
and
\beta\eqqcolon dz^{d-2}/2
for notational transparency.

Let us briefly provide some insight about the table above. First, one notices that
b_{1}
is equal to
-d(d-2)/(d-1)^{2}a^{d-2}
, and this gives the correct answer in all the cases that we have studied. Moreover, the number of variables
b_{i}
is given by

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{d}p(i)-5\,,

(E.4)

where
p(i)
is equal to the number of integer partitions of
i
. Finally, their evaluation in
d
dimensions is accompanied by a discouraging fact. When the
d
-dimensional deformation is considered, the leading order term, which is proportional to
\tr T^{d}
, introduces the “highest order” variable
y_{d^{2}}
, in other words any
y_{i}
with
i>d^{2}
does not enter the analysis. Due to the Cayley-Hamilton theorem (
B.7
), the independent variables are
y_{i}
with
1\leq i\leq d
which means that in our case one needs to re-express the
d(d-1)
variables in terms of the independent ones. This approach is quite tedious and is not very productive for large
d
. Surprisingly, the solution (
4.30
) eliminates this problem completely. To fully understand this fact, let us once more consider the source of the term
y_{d^{2}}
but now from the massive gravity point of view. Taking a closer look to the solution, we observe that the term proportional to
\det Y_{1}
contains all variables from
y_{1}
up to and including
y_{d}
. In the process of evaluating the stress-tensor, we vary this term with respect to the background vielbein and the contribution from
y_{d}
is something proportional to
Y_{d}
. Then, evaluating
\tr T^{d}
leads to the term
y_{d^{2}}
. It is now obvious that the extra
d(d-1)
variables are coming entirely from
\det Y_{1}
. But we mentioned multiple times that this term decouples completely from the background and becomes a
\lambda
-dependent part of the seed theory, therefore by pushing
\det Y_{1}
out of the massive gravity action and evaluating the stress-tensor, the
d(d-1)
variables identically vanish from the
d
-dimensional deforming operator. Their effect is essentially reduced to the addition of the term
-db_{0}\mathfrak{c_{1}}^{\!\!\!d}\det Y_{1}/\lambda^{2}
on the left-hand side of equation (
2.7
).
